  1. [2611.23458] 变指数双非线性方程弱解的局部有界性
    On the local boundedness of weak solutions to doubly nonlinear equations with variable exponents
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究变指数双非线性抛物方程弱子解的局部有界性。
    • 采用对数连续性条件及指数间关系进行分析。
    • 得到局部有界性与定量估计，某些范围需额外可积性假设。
    • 推广了双非线性方程正则性理论。
    💡 研究变指数双非线性抛物方程弱子解的局部有界性，并给出定量估计。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文建立了一类双非线性抛物方程非负弱子解的局部有界性及定量估计，其原型为 (u^q)_t - div(|Du|^{p(x,t)-2}Du)=0，其中 p(x,t)>1，q>0。结果在变指数 p(x,t) 满足对数连续性条件以及 q 与 p(x,t) 局部界之间适当关系的假设下获得。在指数某些范围内，还需要额外的局部可积性假设。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We establish local boundedness, together with quantitative estimates, for non-negative weak sub-solutions to a class of doubly nonlinear parabolic equations whose prototype is \[ (u^q)_t-\operatorname{div} \Big(|Du|^{p(x,t)-2}Du\Big)=0, \qquad p(x,t)>1,\quad q>0. \] The results are obtained under a logarithmic continuity condition on the variable exponent $p(x,t)$ and suitable relations between $q$ and the local bounds of $p(x,t)$. In certain ranges of the exponents, an additional local integrability assumption is required.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23458
  2. [2611.23679] 带诺伊曼边界条件的有限图上的Kazdan-Warner方程
    The Kazdan-Warner equation on a finite graph with a Neumann boundary condition
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究有限图区域上带诺伊曼边界条件的Kazdan-Warner方程。
    • 利用变分方法和上下解技术。
    • 根据参数 c 与 c_g 的关系给出完整可解性刻画。
    • 将经典结果推广到图上的离散设定。
    💡 研究有限图区域上带诺伊曼边界条件的Kazdan-Warner方程的可解性。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究局部有限图连通区域 Ω 上的Kazdan-Warner方程 Δu = c - h e^u，边界条件为 ∂u/∂ν = g。将边界条件化为齐次形式，利用变分方法及上下解研究可解性。令 c_g = |Ω|^{-1}∫_{∂Ω} g dμ，ψ 满足 Δψ = c_g 及边界条件且均值为零。当 c=c_g 且 h 不恒为零时，解存在当且仅当 h 变号且 ∫_Ω h e^ψ dμ < 0。当 c>c_g 时，解存在当且仅当 h 在 Ω 某处为正。当 c<c_g 时，可解性要求 ∫_Ω h e^ψ dμ < 0，此时存在阈值 c_-(h) 使得 c_-(h)<c<c_g 时解存在，c<c_-(h) 时无解。若 h≤0 且不恒为零，则对任意 c<c_g 解存在。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the Kazdan-Warner equation $\Delta u = c - h \mathrm{e}^u$ on a finite connected domain $\Omega$ of a locally finite graph, with Neumann boundary data $\partial u / \partial\nu = g$. We reduce the boundary condition to homogeneous form and use variational methods and upper and lower solutions to study solvability. Let $c_g \mathrel{:=} |\Omega|^{-1}\int_{\partial\Omega} g\, \mathrm{d}\mu$, and let $\psi$ solve $\Delta\psi = c_g$ with boundary data $\partial\psi / \partial\nu = g$ and zero mean on $\Omega$. When $c = c_g$ and $h \not\equiv 0$, a solution exists if and only if $h$ changes sign and $\int_\Omega h \mathrm{e}^\psi\, \mathrm{d}\mu < 0$. When $c > c_g$, a solution exists if and only if $h$ is positive somewhere in $\Omega$. When $c < c_g$, solvability requires $\int_\Omega h \mathrm{e}^\psi\, \mathrm{d}\mu < 0$. Under this condition there is a threshold $-\infty \le c_-(h) < c_g$ such that a solution exists for $c_-(h) < c < c_g$ and no solution exists for $c < c_-(h)$. If $h \le 0$ and $h \not\equiv 0$, a solution exists for every $c < c_g$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23679
  3. [2611.23734] 一维非局部特征值问题的完全分类
    A Complete Classification of the One-Dimensional Nonlocal Eigenvalue Problem
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究一维非局部特征值问题的谱稳定性分类。
    • 利用谱权重累积估计分析虚轴穿越。
    • 给出偶子空间上严格稳定的完整参数区域。
    • 为反应扩散系统斑图稳定性提供理论依据。
    💡 对一维非局部特征值问题的谱稳定性给出完全分类。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文对一维非局部特征值问题 ∂_x^2φ - φ + p w^{p-1}φ - γ(p-1)(∫_R w^{r-1}φ dx / ∫_R w^r dx) w^p = λφ 的谱稳定性给出完全分类，其中 w 是 w''-w+w^p=0 的唯一正偶衰减解，p,r>1，γ∈C 且 Re γ≥0。此类问题常出现于反应扩散系统的斑图形成。在偶子空间上，当 r=(p+3)/2 时严格谱稳定性恰好对 Re γ>1 成立。在 γ 平面中，当 r>(p+3)/2 时两条共轭边界弧从 γ=1 向左展开，严格稳定区域位于其右侧；当 r<(p+3)/2 时弧向右展开，稳定区域位于其间。其公共端点为 γ=1（当 r≥(p-1)/2）或 γ=γ_H>1（当 1<r<(p-1)/2）。每种情形下两弧趋于同一垂直渐近线。作为应用，对实 γ≥0，严格谱稳定的条件为 γ>1（当 r≥(p-1)/2）或 γ>γ_H（当 1<r<(p-1)/2）。关键工具是对混合符号谱权重的累积估计。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We give a complete classification of the spectral stability of the following one-dimensional nonlocal eigenvalue problem (NLEP) $$ \partial_x^2\phi-\phi+pw^{p-1}\phi-\gamma(p-1)\frac{\int_{\mathbb{R}}w^{r-1}\phi dx}{\int_{\mathbb{R}}w^r dx}w^p=\lambda\phi, $$ where $w$ is the unique positive even solution of $w''-w+w^p=0$ decaying at infinity, $p,r>1$, and $\gamma\in\mathbb{C}$ with $\Re\gamma\ge0$. This type of NLEP appears frequently in pattern formation in reaction-diffusion systems. We show that on the even subspace, strict spectral stability holds exactly for $\Re\gamma>1$ when $r=(p+3)/2$. In the $\gamma$-plane, when $r>(p+3)/2$, two conjugate boundary arcs open to the left from $\gamma=1$, and the strictly stable region lies to their right. When $r<(p+3)/2$, the arcs open to the right, and the strictly stable region lies between them. Their common endpoint is $\gamma=1$ when $r\ge(p-1)/2$ and $\gamma=\gamma_H>1$ when $1<r<(p-1)/2$. In each case, the two arcs approach a common vertical asymptote. As an application, for real $\gamma\ge0$, the condition for strict spectral stability is $\gamma>1$ when $r\ge(p-1)/2$ and $\gamma>\gamma_H$ when $1<r<(p-1)/2$, where $\gamma_H>1$ is the value at which a conjugate pair of eigenvalues crosses the imaginary axis. The key ingredient in determining how the number of eigenvalues in the open right half-plane changes at imaginary-axis crossings is a cumulative estimate for the mixed-sign spectral weights that arise for certain $p$ and $r$ with $1<p<3$ and $1<r<2$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23734
  4. [2611.23741] 平面三次-五次NLS方程的非径向阈值散射
    Nonradial threshold scattering for the planar cubic--quintic NLS
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究平面三次-五次NLS方程在阈值质量处的非径向散射。
    • 结合局部Morawetz估计与移动半空间传播估计。
    • 证明阈值质量处双向散射，确定尖锐质量范围。
    • 解决了Murphy提出的公开问题。
    💡 证明平面三次-五次NLS方程在基态质量阈值处的非径向散射。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究平面三次-五次非线性薛定谔方程 i∂_t u + Δu = -|u|^2u + |u|^4u，初始数据 u_0∈H^1_x(R^2)。证明当 u_0 的质量等于聚焦三次方程基态质量时，解在两个时间方向均散射。这解决了Murphy提出的非径向阈值散射问题。结合已知的次阈值散射理论及更大质量驻波的存在性，确定了所有 H^1 解散射的尖锐质量范围。证明结合局部相互作用Morawetz估计与移动半空间上的传播估计来控制外部动能。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the planar cubic--quintic nonlinear Schr\"odinger equation \[ i\partial_tu+\Delta u=-|u|^2u+|u|^4u, \qquad (t,x)\in\mathbb R\times\mathbb R^2, \] with initial data $u_0\in H^1_x(\mathbb R^2)$. We prove scattering in both time directions when the mass of \(u_0\) equals the mass of the ground state for the focusing cubic equation. This resolves the nonradial threshold scattering problem posed by Murphy [\emph{Comm.\ Partial Differential Equations} \textbf{46} (2021), 2213--2234]. Together with the known subthreshold scattering theory and the existence of standing waves at every larger mass, this identifies the sharp mass range in which every $H^1$ solution scatters. The proof combines localized interaction Morawetz estimates with propagation estimates on moving halfspaces to control exterior kinetic energy.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23741
  5. [2611.23797] 所有剩余情形下薛定谔方程的尖锐正交Strichartz估计
    Sharp orthonormal Strichartz estimates for the Schr\"odinger equation in all remaining cases
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究自由薛定谔方程的正交Strichartz估计。
    • 解决系数最优可和性的剩余开放情形。
    • 完成 0≤s<d/2 的完整分类。
    • 完善了正交Strichartz估计理论。
    💡 解决自由薛定谔方程正交Strichartz估计中剩余的最优可和性情形。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究齐次Sobolev空间 Ḣ^s 中自由薛定谔方程的正交Strichartz估计。解决了关于系数最优可和性的剩余开放情形。结合已有结果与反例，本文定理完成了非负Sobolev正则性 0≤s<d/2 的完整分类。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study orthonormal Strichartz estimates for the free Schr\"odinger equation in homogeneous Sobolev spaces $\dot H^s$. We resolve the remaining open cases concerning the optimal summability of the coefficients. Together with earlier results and counterexamples, our theorems complete the classification for nonnegative Sobolev regularity $0\le s<d/2$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23797
  6. [2611.23902] 低正则性Boltzmann与BGK方程向平衡态的构造性趋势
    Constructive Trend to Equilibrium for Low-regularity Boltzmann and BGK equations
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究低正则性Boltzmann和BGK方程解的长时间行为。
    • 利用自由输运控制熵的有限时间估计。
    • 得到多项式及指数衰减，并建立密度准则。
    • 为低正则性动理学理论提供新工具。
    💡 研究低正则性下Boltzmann和BGK方程全局温和解向平衡态的长时间行为。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究 T^3 上截断Boltzmann和BGK方程全局温和解的长时间行为，假设高斯下界和多项式加权 L^∞ 上界。主要步骤是利用自由输运控制局部Maxwellian熵的有限时间估计，无需Sobolev正则性。对Boltzmann方程，Villani熵产生不等式和碰撞核比较给出速度权 m>7 时的多项式衰减；权足够大时大振幅理论给出加权范数和相对熵的指数衰减。对BGK方程，微观不等式为线性，熵从有限时间估计指数衰减。最后建立大数据的密度准则。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the long-time behavior of global mild solutions to the cutoff Boltzmann and BGK equations on $\T^3$ under a Gaussian lower bound and a polynomially weighted $L^\infty$ upper bound. The main step is a finite-time estimate which uses free transport to control the entropy of the local Maxwellian by microscopic entropy and dissipation, without assuming Sobolev regularity. For Boltzmann, Villani's entropy-production inequality and a comparison of collision kernels give polynomial decay when the velocity weight has order $m>7$. For a sufficiently large weight, the large-amplitude theory then gives exponential decay in the weighted norm and in relative entropy. For BGK, the microscopic inequality is linear, so entropy decays exponentially from the finite-time estimate. Finally, we establish a density criterion for large-data BGK dynamics in the polynomially weighted $L^\infty$ class of Perthame and Pulvirenti. For weight order $m>10$, a uniform upper bound on the local density guarantees uniform-in-time weighted bounds and exponential convergence to equilibrium in both relative entropy and the weighted $L^\infty$ norm.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23902
  7. [2611.23912] 一维Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程稀疏波-接触间断-激波复合波的时间衰减率
    Time-Decay Rates toward a Rarefaction-Contact-Shock Composite Wave for the One-Dimensional Navier-Stokes-Fourier Equations
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究一维Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程复合波的稳定性。
    • 采用能量估计与有限时间比较方法。
    • 得到复合波的时间衰减率及逐点界。
    • 深化了可压缩流体复合波稳定性理论。
    💡 研究一维Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程复合波的时间衰减率。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文考虑一维可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程，其两端状态由稀疏波、接触间断和激波连接。对固定的弱非零激波、足够弱的稀疏波和接触波以及小 H^2(R) 初始扰动，建立带时间依赖位移的粘性激波渐近稳定性。证明 [t,2t] 上一致扰动和位移速度的 L^4 时间平均以 C_S(1+t)^{-1/4} 为界，并给出全空间 (1+t)^{-1/12}、激波附近 (1+t)^{-1/4} 及 |x|≥ηt 上 (1+t)^{-1/6} 的逐点界。证明使用能量估计和有限时间区间上的比较。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We consider the one-dimensional compressible Navier-Stokes-Fourier equations when the end states are connected by a rarefaction wave, a contact discontinuity, and a shock. For a fixed weak nonzero shock, sufficiently weak rarefaction and contact waves, and small H^2(R) initial perturbations, we establish asymptotic stability with a time-dependent shift on the viscous shock. The L^4 time average of the uniform perturbation and the shift velocity over [t,2t] is bounded by C_S(1+t)^(-1/4). We prove pointwise-in-time bounds of order (1+t)^(-1/12) on the whole line, (1+t)^(-1/4) near the shifted shock, and (1+t)^(-1/6) on |x| >= \eta t for each fixed \eta>0. The proof uses energy estimates and comparison on finite time interval

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23912
  8. [2611.23937] R^2上仅具垂直耗散的Navier-Stokes与不可压多孔介质方程的稳定性
    Stability for the Navier--Stokes and Incompressible Porous Media Equations with Only Vertical Dissipation on $\mathbb{R}^2$
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究仅垂直耗散下二维Navier-Stokes和IPM方程的稳定性。
    • 结合奇函数加权不等式与各向异性能量估计。
    • 得到全局H^2稳定性及代数衰减。
    • 揭示了垂直耗散对稳定性的关键作用。
    💡 证明仅垂直耗散下二维Navier-Stokes和IPM方程的小数据全局稳定性与衰减。
    📖 中文摘要

    对 R^2 上二维Navier-Stokes方程，证明仅垂直粘性即可对具有多项式加权涡量的小反射对称数据给出全局 H^2 稳定性和涡量、速度的代数衰减。在更强小性假设下得到 H^3 稳定性和涡量梯度衰减，与同对称类Euler流的无界梯度增长形成对比。对不可压多孔介质方程，在类似假设下仅垂直扩散即得 H^2 稳定性和衰减，尽管Darcy律无导数增益。这补充了周期域上无扩散IPM的有限时间爆破构造。证明结合奇函数的加权不等式与利用各系统结构的各向异性能量估计。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations on $\mathbb{R}^2$, we prove that vertical viscosity alone yields global $H^2$ stability and algebraic decay of the vorticity and velocity for small reflection-symmetric data with polynomially weighted vorticity. Under stronger smallness assumptions, we obtain $H^3$ stability and vorticity-gradient decay, in contrast to unbounded gradient growth for Euler flows in the same symmetry class. For incompressible porous media (IPM), we obtain $H^2$ stability and decay under analogous assumptions with only vertical diffusion, despite the absence of derivative gain in Darcy's law. This complements finite-time blow-up constructions for nondiffusive IPM in periodic domains. The proofs combine a weighted inequality for odd functions with anisotropic energy estimates exploiting the structure of each system.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23937
  9. [2611.23995] 二维Hamiltonian abcd Boussinesq系统小径向解的局部能量衰减
    Local energy decay for small radial solutions of the two-dimensional Hamiltonian $abcd$ Boussinesq system
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究二维Hamiltonian abcd Boussinesq系统小径向解的长时间行为。
    • 利用新的位力恒等式和修正泛函。
    • 证明局部能量衰减及不存在小径向周期解。
    • 首次描述二维abcd系统的长时间动力学。
    💡 证明二维Hamiltonian abcd Boussinesq系统小径向解的局部能量衰减。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文考虑二维 abcd Boussinesq系统，这是描述小振幅长表面波双向传播的四参数模型族，处于一般区域 a,c<0，b,d>0。首先重新审视 2≤n≤4 维Sobolev空间中的局部适定性，证明Hamiltonian情形 b=d 时Cauchy问题在能量空间 H^1(R^n) 中对小数据全局适定。然后考虑 n=2 时全局解的渐近行为。在 -a,-c>b/9 条件下，证明每个小径向解具有局部能量衰减，从而不存在小径向时间周期解。证明基于适应径向结构的新的位力恒等式，并引入两个修正泛函使二次型在 -a,-c>b/9 时强制，该阈值对泛函是尖锐的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We consider the $abcd$ Boussinesq system in two space dimensions, a four-parameter family of models for the two-way propagation of small-amplitude long surface waves, in the generic regime $a,c<0$, $b,d>0$. We first revisit the local well-posedness theory in Sobolev spaces in dimensions $2\le n\le4$ and show that for the Hamiltonian case $b=d$ the Cauchy problem is globally well-posed for small data in the energy space $\mathbb{H}^1(\mathbb R^n)$. Then we consider the asymptotic behavior of these global solutions in the $n=2$ case. Under the condition $-a,-c>b/9$, we prove that every small solution with radial symmetry exhibits local energy decay. As a consequence, there are no small radial time-periodic (in particular, stationary) solutions. Decay also holds, along sequences of times, on balls whose radius grows almost linearly in time. The proof is based on new virial identities adapted to the radial structure of the system. In the proof we introduce two correction functionals such that the resulting quadratic form is coercive precisely when $-a,-c>b/9$, a threshold which is sharp for the functional. To our knowledge this is the first result describing the long-time dynamics of $abcd$ systems in dimension two.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23995
  10. [2611.24027] A3s MTW条件下最优传输势的边界C^{2,γ}正则性
    Boundary $ C^{2, \gamma} $ regularity for optimal transport potentials under the A3s MTW condition
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究A3s MTW条件下最优传输势的边界正则性。
    • 推广Collins-Tong单调性公式至一般代价。
    • 证明势的C^{2,γ}及传输映射的C^{1,γ}正则性。
    • 推进了最优传输正则性理论。
    💡 在A3s MTW条件下建立最优传输势的边界C^{2,γ}正则性。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文在A3s Ma-Trudinger-Wang条件下建立最优传输势的边界正则性。设源和目标为 R^n（n≥2）中有界互 c-凸区域，边界为 C^{1,β}，代价函数光滑、双扭曲且非退化。对正 C^{0,α} 且远离零、总质量相等的密度，证明两个Kantorovich势属于到边界的 C^{2,γ}，其中 γ=min{α,β}。因此最优传输映射到边界为 C^{1,γ}。证明通过Kim-McCann度量几何将Collins-Tong的单调性公式方法推广到一般代价，所得公式给出爆破极限的齐次性和截面的均匀估计，进而得到边界斜率和与常密度Monge-Ampère方程解的比较。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We establish boundary regularity for optimal transport potentials under the A3s Ma--Trudinger--Wang condition. Let the source and target be bounded, mutually $ c $-convex domains in $ \mathbb{R}^n $, $ n\geqslant2 $, with $ C^{1,\beta} $ boundaries, and suppose that the cost is smooth, bi-twisted, and nondegenerate. For positive $ C^{0,\alpha} $ densities bounded away from zero and having equal total mass, we prove that both Kantorovich potentials belong to $ C^{2,\gamma} $ up to the boundary, where $ \gamma = \min\{\alpha,\beta\} $. Consequently, the optimal transport map is $ C^{1,\gamma} $ up to the boundary. The proof extends the monotonicity formula method of Collins and Tong to general costs through the geometry of the Kim--McCann metric. The resulting formula yields homogeneity of blow-up limits and uniform estimates for sections. These estimates give boundary obliqueness and allow comparison with solutions of the constant-density Monge--Amp\`ere equation, leading to the claimed regularity.

  11. [2611.24046] 重力水波的最优局部梯度估计及其在Stokes猜想中的应用
    Optimal local gradient estimate for gravity water waves and its application to the Stokes conjecture
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究重力水波自由边界问题与Stokes猜想。
    • 推广Hopf微分方法并引入加权Cauchy-Beurling修正。
    • 建立最优局部梯度估计，去除先验增长假设。
    • 推进了Stokes猜想的几何分析证明。
    💡 建立重力水波自由边界问题的最优局部梯度估计，并应用于Stokes猜想。
    📖 中文摘要

    1880年Stokes研究二维重力水波并猜想波峰在驻点处形成角为2π/3的对称角。该猜想由Amick-Fraenkel-Toland和Plotnikov通过Nekrasov积分方程证明，但依赖驻点孤立性及波剖面对称单调性等结构假设。2011年Varvaruca-Weiss引入新几何方法，在二维流函数局部梯度界下给出不依赖上述假设的新证明。本文对自由边界问题的一般变分解建立最优局部梯度估计，将De Philippis-Hirsch-Nahon的Hopf微分方法推广到重力情形，并结合其抽象逼近定理。关键新观察是加权Cauchy-Beurling修正与下半平面消失性结合提供所需高度衰减。由此去除[Acta Math. 206 (2011)]中的先验增长假设，并对一般变分解建立Stokes角或全密度爆破二择一。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In 1880, G. Stokes [Mathematical and Physical Papers, Vol. I] studied a two-dimensional gravity water wave and conjectured that the wave crest forms a symmetric corner of angle 2pi/3 at the stagnation point. The conjecture was first proved by Amick, Fraenkel, and Toland [Acta Math. 148 (1982)] and, independently, by Plotnikov [Dinamika Sploshn. Sredy 57 (1982)] via the Nekrasov integral equation. Their proofs rely on structural assumptions, including the isolation of stagnation points and the symmetry and monotonicity of the wave profile. In 2011, Varvaruca and Weiss [Acta Math. 206 (2011)] introduced a novel geometric approach and gave a new proof of the conjecture without relying on the structural assumptions mentioned above, under a local gradient bound for the two-dimensional stream function. In this paper, we establish an optimal local gradient estimate for general variational solutions of the free boundary problem. Our approach adapts the Hopf differential method of De Philippis, Hirsch, and Nahon [arXiv:2609.12180v2] to the gravitational setting, and combines it with their abstract approximation theorem to turn the holomorphic approximation of the Hopf differential into the local gradient bounds. An important new observation is that the weighted Cauchy-Beurling correction, combined with vanishing on the lower half plane, supplies the additional height decay needed to obtain the local gradient bounds. As a consequence, we remove the a priori growth hypothesis from the geometric analysis in [Acta Math. 206 (2011)]. For general variational solutions, we establish the Stokes corner or full-density blow-up alternative. Under the stated topological and interface hypotheses, we obtain the Stokes corner and two free boundary branches that are C^1 up to the stagnation point.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24046

    • 证明迹的Lipschitz性、C^1及实解析性。
    • 结果对光滑数据最优，完善了极小曲面边界理论。
    💡 研究解析Dirichlet数据下平面极小曲面方程广义解的尖锐边界正则性。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究有界集 Ω 上极小曲面方程Dirichlet问题广义BV解的边界行为。广义解在 Ω 内总是光滑函数 v 的图像，但可能在 ∂Ω 上带有垂直部分，记录给定Dirichlet数据 g 与 v 的边界迹 h 之间的差距。当 Ω 为有界实解析平面区域且 g 实解析时，证明 h 为Lipschitz、每点左右可微、在 ∂Ω\Σ 上 C^1、在 {h≠g}\Σ 上实解析，其中 Σ={κ=0} 为边界曲率零点集。在 p∈Σ∩{h≠g} 处两支可实解析延拓。证明将接触点附近的垂直部分转化为右端可能退化的标量障碍问题，模型情形为 Δv=H·1_{v>0}。结果最优：光滑数据下迹可能不连续，实解析情形角点可出现。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the boundary behavior of generalized (BV) solutions of the Dirichlet problem for the minimal surface equation over a bounded set \(\Omega\). Such a generalized solution is always the graph of a smooth function \(v\) inside \(\Omega\), but might carry a vertical part over \(\partial\Omega\), which records the gap between the prescribed Dirichlet datum \(g\) and the boundary trace \(h\) attained by \(v\). When \(\Omega\) is a bounded real-analytic planar domain and \(g\) is real analytic we prove that \(h\) is Lipschitz, right and left differentiable at every point, of class \(C^1\) on \(\partial\Omega\setminus\Sigma\), and real analytic on \(\{h\neq g\}\setminus\Sigma\), where \(\Sigma=\{\kappa=0\}\) is the zero set of the curvature of \(\partial\Omega\). Also, at each point \(p\in\Sigma\cap\{h\neq g\}\), the two one-sided branches extend real analytically across \(p\). The proof recasts the vertical part near a contact point as a scalar obstacle problem with a possibly degenerate right-hand side, whose model case is \(\Delta v=H\,\id_{\{v>0\}}\) with \(H\ge0\) a polynomial. These results are optimal: for merely smooth data the trace may be discontinuous, while in the real-analytic case there are examples where corners do occur.

  15. [2611.24224] 时间可积强迫下的Fujita方程：有效质量、质量湮灭与尖锐生命跨度
    The Fujita equation with time-integrable forcing: effective mass, mass annihilation and sharp lifespan
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究时间可积强迫下Fujita方程的解行为。
    • 引入有效质量并证明精确质量湮灭恒等式。
    • 给出全局存在性、渐近质量及尖锐生命跨度。
    • 将Meier定理推广到非局部设定。
    💡 研究时间可积强迫下Fujita方程的有效质量、质量湮灭及尖锐生命跨度。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究 Cauchy 问题 ∂_t u - Δu = |u|^p + ζ(t)w(x)（R^N×(0,∞)，p>1），数据变号，强迫时间剖面 ζ 在 (0,∞) 上可积，模型情形 ζ(t)=t^σ e^{-t}（σ>-1）。对幂型剖面 ζ(t)=t^σ，已知强迫会提高临界指数或破坏全局存在。本文证明可积强迫不改变 Fujita 指数 p_F=1+2/N，仅通过有效质量 M_eff=∫u_0+(∫_0^∞ζ)(∫w) 起作用。核心结果是精确质量湮灭恒等式：若 1<p≤p_F，每个全局弱解属于 L^p(R^N×(0,∞)) 且 ∬|u|^p=-M_eff。因此 p≤p_F 时，若 M_eff≥0 且数据非平凡则无全局解，且每个全局温和解在 L^1 中消失。对 p>p_F，在单一积分条件下解被线性强迫流控制，给出无符号限制的小数据全局存在性及渐近质量 m_∞=M_eff+∬|u|^p。还给出所有 p>1 的局部必要条件和尖锐生命跨度估计。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the Cauchy problem $\partial_t u-\Delta u=|u|^p+\zeta(t)w(x)$ in $\mathbb{R}^N\times(0,\infty)$, $p>1$, with sign-changing data and a forcing whose temporal profile $\zeta$ is integrable on $(0,\infty)$, the model case being $\zeta(t)=t^\sigma e^{-t}$, $\sigma>-1$. For power-type profiles $\zeta(t)=t^\sigma$ the forcing is known to raise the critical exponent above the Fujita exponent $p_F=1+2/N$ or to destroy global existence. We show that an integrable forcing leaves $p_F$ unchanged and acts only through the effective mass $M_{\rm eff}=\int u_0+(\int_0^\infty\zeta)(\int w)$. Our central result is an exact mass-annihilation identity: if $1<p\le p_F$, every global weak solution lies in $L^p(\mathbb{R}^N\times(0,\infty))$ and satisfies $\iint|u|^p=-M_{\rm eff}$. Hence, for $p\le p_F$, no global solution exists when $M_{\rm eff}\ge0$ and the data are nontrivial, and every global mild solution vanishes in $L^1$ as $t\to\infty$; examples show that $M_{\rm eff}<0$ neither excludes nor implies global existence. For $p>p_F$, every solution is dominated by a multiple of the linear forced flow under a single integral condition; this gives small-data global existence without sign restrictions and, if $\zeta$ decays mildly, convergence to $m_\infty G_t$ with asymptotic mass $m_\infty=M_{\rm eff}+\iint|u|^p$. We also give a localized necessary condition valid for all $p>1$, showing that large or slowly decaying data blow up regardless of the forcing and that non-integrable forcings shift the threshold, and sharp lifespan estimates for small data with positive effective mass: $T_\varepsilon\simeq\varepsilon^{-2(p-1)/(2-N(p-1))}$ for $p<p_F$ and $\log T_\varepsilon\simeq\varepsilon^{-(p-1)}$ for $p=p_F$.

  18. [2611.24340] 具慢变时间依赖源的时间-空间分数阶反应扩散方程的临界指数
    On the Critical Exponent for a Time--Space Fractional Reaction--Diffusion Equation with a Slowly Varying Time-Dependent Source
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究时间-空间分数阶反应扩散方程的Fujita临界指数。
    • 利用Karamata慢变函数分析时间依赖源。
    • 给出完整分类及临界情形下的二分法。
    • 将Meier定理推广到非局部时间-空间分数阶设定。
    💡 研究具慢变时间依赖源的时间-空间分数阶反应扩散方程的Fujita临界指数。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究 Cauchy 问题 ∂_t^α u + (-Δ)^{θ/2}u = h(t)u^p（(0,∞)×R^N，u(0)=u_0≥0），其中 α∈(0,1]，θ∈(0,2]，p>1，∂_t^α 为Caputo导数，h(t)~t^γℓ(t)（t 大时），γ∈R，ℓ 为Karamata慢变函数。对 α=1 及所有 θ∈(0,2] 给出完整分类：若 γ≤-1，小数据对任意 p>1 全局存在，无Fujita阈值；若 γ>-1，临界指数 p_c(γ)=1+θ(γ+1)/N，1<p<p_c 时非平凡解爆破，p>p_c 时小数据全局存在；在 p=p_c 时慢变因子决定：∫_1^∞ t^{-1}ℓ(t)dt=∞ 则爆破，收敛则小数据全局存在。对 α∈(0,1)，Caputo导数的记忆将 γ+1 替换为 γ+α。证明 γ≤-α 时对任意 p>1 小数据全局存在；γ>-α 时 1<p<p_c(γ,α):=1+θ(γ+α)/(Nα) 时所有非平凡解爆破。若 N<θ，p>p_c 时也全局存在，分类完整；若 N≥θ，得到 p>1+(γ+α)/α 的全局存在性。还给出局部爆破机制表明 N>θ 且 γ>αθ/(N-θ) 时 p_c(γ,α) 非Fujita指数。结果将Meier定理推广到非局部设定。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the Fujita critical exponent for the Cauchy problem $\partial_t^\alpha u+(-\Delta)^{\theta/2}u=h(t)u^p$ in $(0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^N$, $u(0)=u_0\ge0$, where $\alpha\in(0,1]$, $\theta\in(0,2]$, $p>1$, $\partial_t^\alpha$ is the Caputo derivative, and $h(t)\sim t^\gamma\ell(t)$ for large $t$, with $\gamma\in\mathbb{R}$ and $\ell$ slowly varying at infinity in the sense of Karamata. For $\alpha=1$ and every $\theta\in(0,2]$ we obtain a complete classification. If $\gamma\le-1$, small data give global solutions for every $p>1$, so there is no Fujita threshold. If $\gamma>-1$, the critical exponent is $p_c(\gamma)=1+\theta(\gamma+1)/N$: every nontrivial solution blows up for $1<p<p_c(\gamma)$, while small global solutions exist for $p>p_c(\gamma)$. At $p=p_c(\gamma)$ the slowly varying factor decides: blow-up occurs if $\int_1^\infty t^{-1}\ell(t)dt=\infty$, and small global solutions exist if this integral converges. Hence global existence may occur at the critical exponent, unlike the autonomous case. For $\alpha\in(0,1)$, the memory of the Caputo derivative replaces $\gamma+1$ by $\gamma+\alpha$. We prove small-data global existence for every $p>1$ when $\gamma\le-\alpha$. When $\gamma>-\alpha$, all nontrivial solutions blow up for $1<p<p_c(\gamma,\alpha):=1+\theta(\gamma+\alpha)/(N\alpha)$. If $N<\theta$, we also obtain global existence for $p>p_c(\gamma,\alpha)$, so the classification is complete, and at $p=p_c(\gamma,\alpha)$ the dichotomy is governed by the boundedness of $\ell$. If $N\ge\theta$, we obtain global existence for $p>1+(\gamma+\alpha)/\alpha$, which coincides with $p_c(\gamma,\alpha)$ when $N=\theta$. Finally, a second, purely local blow-up mechanism shows that for $N>\theta$ and $\gamma>\alpha\theta/(N-\theta)$, $p_c(\gamma,\alpha)$ is not the Fujita exponent. Our results extend the theorems of Meier (1990) to the nonlocal setting.

    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究 R^3 上具分数阶水平耗散 νΛ_h^{2α}（0<α≤1）和Coriolis力的三维不可压Navier-Stokes方程，无垂直耗散。缩放临界Sobolev指数为 s_c=5/2-2α。对 0<α<1，证明 s≥s_c 时 H^s 中局部Hadamard适定性；对 α=1，范围为 s>1/2。在每个给定有限旋转速率下，对光滑紧支无散初始数据，经典解在其余正阶Sobolev空间中发生范数膨胀，可从任意小 H^s 数据在任意短时间内开始。因此 0<α<1 时 H^{s_c} 是适定空间，而 α=1 时 H^{1/2} 出现范数膨胀。这些结果给出每个有限旋转速率下的尖锐Sobolev阈值。在整个适定范围内，小数据给出全局解且小性阈值与旋转无关，每个固定数据在足够快旋转下给出全局解。非线性解在 [0,∞) 上一致收敛到线性Stokes-Coriolis演化。对谱局部化数据，收敛速率为 O(|Ω|^{-γ})（0<γ<1/2）。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes equations on $\mathbb R^3$ with fractional horizontal dissipation $\nu\Lambda_h^{2\alpha}$, $0<\alpha\le1$, and a Coriolis force. There is no vertical dissipation. The scaling-critical Sobolev index is $s_c=5/2-2\alpha$. For $0<\alpha<1$, we prove local Hadamard well-posedness in $H^s$ for $s\ge s_c$; for $\alpha=1$, the range is $s>1/2$. At every prescribed finite rotation rate, norm inflation holds in all remaining positive-order Sobolev spaces for classical solutions with smooth compactly supported divergence-free initial data, starting from arbitrarily small $H^s$ data in arbitrarily short time. Thus $H^{s_c}$ is a well-posedness space for $0<\alpha<1$, whereas $H^{1/2}$ exhibits norm inflation for $\alpha=1$. These results give sharp Sobolev thresholds for each finite rotation rate. Throughout the well-posedness range, small data yield global solutions with a rotation-independent smallness threshold, and every fixed datum yields a global solution for sufficiently rapid rotation. The nonlinear solution converges to the linear Stokes--Coriolis evolution uniformly on $[0,\infty)$ and in the global dissipation norm as $|\Omega|\to\infty$. For spectrally localized data separated from the vertical frequency axis, the convergence rate is $O(|\Omega|^{-\gamma})$ for every $0<\gamma<1/2$. For $0<\alpha<1$, the energy estimates close at $s=s_c$ without loss of derivatives in isotropic Sobolev spaces, using Littlewood--Paley decomposition, Sobolev trace inequalities, and localized horizontal product estimates. Below $s_c$, norm inflation follows from an approximation by a fixed smooth Euler solution after rescaling. At $(\alpha,s)=(1,1/2)$, viscosity remains fixed under critical rescaling; the construction instead uses a localized scalar shear and conormal analytic stability estimates uniform in the vertical concentration parameters.

We also characterize all extremals and prove sharp quantitative stability estimates.     📖 中文摘要

    对每个$n\geq 2$，我们构造有界区域$\Omega\subset\mathbb R^{n+1}$，它是单位球在$\mathbb R^{n+1}$的双Lipschitz同胚下的像，并构造零均值有界Neumann数据$g$，使得Neumann问题的变分解$u$满足$\mathcal N(\nabla u)\notin L^1(\partial\Omega)$。特别地，尽管$\Omega$是双边弦弧域且边界支持弱(1,1)-Poincaré不等式，且在该类区域中Dirichlet问题和正则性问题对某些指数可解，但$L^p$ Neumann问题对任何$1<p<\infty$都不可解。这否定了C.     🌐 English Abstract

    For every $n\geq 2$ we construct a bounded domain $\Omega\subset\mathbb R^{n+1}$, which is the image of the unit ball under a bi-Lipschitz homeomorphism of $\mathbb R^{n+1}$, and a bounded Neumann datum $g$ with zero mean such that the variational solution $u$ of the Neumann problem with datum $g$ satisfies $\mathcal N(\nabla u)\notin L^1(\partial\Omega)$. In particular, the $L^p$ Neumann problem is not solvable in $\Omega$ for any $1<p<\infty$, although $\Omega$ is a two-sided chord-arc domain whose boundary supports a weak $(1,1)$-Poincar\'e inequality, and although in this class of domains the Dirichlet and the regularity problems are solvable for some exponents. This answers in the negative a question of C. Kenig, and it also shows that, unlike the solvability of the Dirichlet problem, the solvability of the $L^p$ Neumann problem for some $p\in (1,\infty)$ is not invariant under bi-Lipschitz homeomorphisms of $\mathbb R^{n+1}$.

    📖 中文摘要

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that for any concave, non-Osgood modulus of continuity $\omega$, there exists a divergence-free velocity field $u\in L^\infty([0,1];C^\omega(\mathbb T^2))$ and an initial data $f_0\in C^\infty(\mathbb T^2)$ such that the solutions of the associated advection-diffusion equation exhibit anomalous dissipation.   30. [2611.24808] 上半空间中散度型椭圆方程$L^p$-Neumann问题
    On the $L^p$-Neumann problem for divergence form elliptic equations in the upper half-space
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究上半空间中散度型椭圆方程$L^p$-Neumann问题的可解性。
    • 构造反例，利用非切向极大函数和Carleson测度分析。
    • 证明两类方程对任何$p$均不可解，即使数据光滑。
    • 表明Neumann问题可解性在Carleson扰动下不稳定。
    💡 否定回答上半空间中两类椭圆方程$L^p$-Neumann问题的可解性。
    📖 中文摘要

    一个核心问题是：对于上半空间$\mathbb{R}^{n+1}_+$中系数独立于横向变量或满足Dahlberg--Kenig--Pipher (DKP)条件的散度型二阶一致椭圆方程，$L^p$-Neumann问题是否对某个$p\in(1,\infty)$可解。我们对两类方程均给出否定回答，即使矩阵的对称部分为单位阵。两个例子具有相同的非零Neumann数据$g\in C_c^\infty(\mathbb R^n)$。尽管$g$有界且属于实Hardy空间$H^1(\mathbb R^n)$，两个方程均不存在弱解使其梯度的非切向极大函数属于$L^p(\mathbb R^n)$对任何$1\leq p<\infty$。对于变分解$u$，$\nabla_x u$、$\partial_tu$和$(A\nabla u)_{n+1}$的非切向极大函数对每个$1\leq p\leq\infty$都有无限$L^p$范数。在系数独立于横向变量的例子中，内部方程为Laplace方程。DKP例子中的矩阵与单位阵有有限$L^2$型Carleson差距。这表明$L^p$-Neumann问题的可解性在单位阵的任意Carleson扰动下不稳定。对于下半空间系数的适当选择，单层位势的切向梯度和横向导数的$L^2$非切向极大估计和一致切片估计均失败。其边界迹的切向梯度的$L^2$估计也失败。我们解释了边界下方系数的作用，并在DKP范数足够小时证明了反射延拓的$L^2$位势估计。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A central question has been whether the $L^p$-Neumann problem is solvable for some $p\in(1,\infty)$ in the upper half-space $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}_+:=\mathbb{R}^n\times(0,\infty)$, $n\geq2$, for second-order uniformly elliptic equations in divergence form whose coefficients are either independent of the transverse variable or satisfy the Dahlberg--Kenig--Pipher (DKP) condition. We answer this question in the negative for both classes, even for matrices whose symmetric part is the identity. The two examples have the same nonzero Neumann datum $g\in C_c^\infty(\mathbb R^n)$ of mean zero. Although $g$ is bounded and belongs to the real Hardy space $H^1(\mathbb R^n)$, neither equation admits a weak solution with this datum whose gradient has a non-tangential maximal function in $L^p(\mathbb R^n)$ for any $1\leq p<\infty$. For the variational solution $u$ associated with either matrix $A$, the non-tangential maximal functions of $\nabla_x u$, $\partial_tu$ and $(A\nabla u)_{n+1}$ each have infinite $L^p$ norm for every $1\leq p\leq\infty$. In the example with coefficients independent of the transverse variable, the equation in the interior is the Laplace equation. The matrix in the DKP example has finite $L^2$-type Carleson discrepancy from the identity. This shows that solvability of the $L^p$-Neumann problem is not stable under arbitrary Carleson perturbations of the identity. For a suitable choice of the coefficients in the lower half-space, the $L^2$ non-tangential maximal and uniform slice estimates fail for both the tangential gradient and the transverse derivative of the associated single layer potential. The $L^2$ estimate for the tangential gradient of its boundary trace also fails. We explain the role of the coefficients below the boundary and prove the $L^2$ layer potential estimates for the reflected extension when the DKP norm is sufficiently small.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24808

We also investigate the strong competition limit, and characterize fully non-trivial limiting profiles as minimizers of a partially segregated variational problem. A preliminary study of independent interest concerns the whole-space problem. Yet subspaces defined by Hessian eigenvalue magnitude, such as the dominant/bulk decomposition underlying Dom-SGD and Bulk-SGD, have repeatedly failed to capture where optimization takes place, and adaptive Krylov-based alternatives built on energy-fraction thresholds show promise but still struggle at lower eigenmodes. We argue this failure is structural: the directions of highest curvature are not, in general, the directions along which training moves. We introduce the dominant aligned subspace (DAS), constructed from a full eigendecomposition of the Hessian but selected by gradient alignment rather than eigenvalue magnitude, as a tool for understanding optimization dynamics and a foundation for designing methods that exploit them. To expose this structure under the hardest conditions, we use physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) as a testbed: differential operators from the parabolic, elliptic, and hyperbolic classes induce severely ill-conditioned, anisotropic loss landscapes. Across Poisson, heat, wave, and viscous Burgers benchmarks DAS-restricted training with a line search matches full-space training even where the projected Hessian remains ill-conditioned, showing empirically that convergence does not require all Hessian eigendirections but only a low-dimensional, gradient-aligned subset. We extend the analysis to Gauss-Newton within the DAS and discuss what this structure implies for future subspace-aware optimizers.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23773

This reduction transforms the resonance problem into a low-dimensional nonlinear eigenvalue problem, avoiding large-scale discretizations and global root-search procedures.     我们为高余维平均曲率流引入二次凸性，它结合了平均曲率方向上的定量凸性与第二基本形式的完全控制。严格Andrews-Baker二次拼挤蕴含二次凸性，且新不等式允许超越临界二次比率的曲率张量。对n≥5的紧连通闭子流形，证明二次凸性保持并在有限时间收敛到圆点。对n≥4且具有球面自由边界的紧连通子流形，证明保持并收敛到唯一(n+1)-平面中的圆半球。这是任意余维球面自由边界MCF的首个此类收敛定理。闭情形定理在5≤n≤7时需要额外二次拼挤，自由边界定理在4≤n≤7时需要。对n≥8，所需二次界由二次凸性推出。我们还证明在球面自由边界流下，n≥2且9/(8n)≤c≤4/(3n)（含端点）时普通二次拼挤|A|^2≤c|H|^2保持。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce quadratic convexity for mean curvature flow in high codimension. It combines quantitative convexity in the mean curvature direction with control of the full second fundamental form. Strict Andrews-Baker quadratic pinching implies quadratic convexity, while the new inequality also admits curvature tensors beyond the critical quadratic ratio. For compact connected closed submanifolds of dimension $n\geq5$, we prove preservation of quadratic convexity and convergence in finite time to a round point. For compact connected submanifolds of dimension $n\geq4$ with spherical free boundary, we prove preservation and convergence to a round hemisphere in a uniquely determined $(n+1)$-plane. To our knowledge, this is the first such convergence theorem for spherical free boundary MCF in arbitrary codimension. The closed theorem requires additional quadratic pinching for $5\leq n\leq7$, and the free boundary theorem for $4\leq n\leq7$. For $n\geq8$, the necessary quadratic bound follows from quadratic convexity. We also establish preservation of ordinary quadratic pinching $|A|^2\leq c|H|^2$ under spherical free boundary flow for $n\ge2$ and $\frac{9}{8n}\leq c\leq\frac{4}{3n}$, including both endpoints.

Combining this implication with existing results for the Monge-Amp\`ere system, we obtain new ${C}^{1,\alpha}$ flexibility statement for isometric immersions with arbitrary source and ambient metrics.     我们证明Pick或Hopf微分的零点阶数通过尖锐圆盘比较控制相应凸区域。对完备双曲仿射球，Pick零点阶数m≥0给出归一化射影坐标，其中区域位于半径√cos(π/(m+3))与其倒数的圆盘之间。对到具有完备∂-能量度量的凸双曲区域的调和微分同胚，Hopf零点阶数m给出像中以像点为中心半径log cot(π/(2(m+2)))的圆盘。等号刻画相应正多边形。对多边形目标，这些比较证明Dumas-Wolf的Fence猜想并给出高阶围栏的显式Hilbert和双曲距离界。还构造单零点例子表明这些充分重数条件非必要。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that the vanishing order of a Pick or Hopf differential controls the corresponding convex domain through sharp disk comparisons. For a complete hyperbolic affine sphere, Pick vanishing order $m\ge0$ gives normalized projective coordinates in which the domain lies between disks of radii $\sqrt{\cos(\pi/(m+3))}$ and its reciprocal. For a harmonic diffeomorphism to a convex hyperbolic domain with complete $\partial$-energy metric, Hopf vanishing order $m$ gives a disk in the image of radius $\log\cot(\pi/(2(m+2)))$, centered at the image point. Equality characterizes the corresponding regular polygons. For polygonal targets, these comparisons prove the Fence conjecture of Dumas--Wolf and give explicit Hilbert and hyperbolic distance bounds for higher fences. We also construct simple-zero examples showing that these sufficient multiplicity conditions are not necessary.

  13. [2611.24090] G_2几何中的耦合流：保持性、存在性与稳定性
    Coupled flows in $G_2$ geometry: preservation, existence and stability
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究G_2几何中保持余结合子流形与瞬子的耦合流。
    • 通过添加Lie导数和椭圆修正克服保持性失败，建立修正耦合系统。
    • 证明短时存在唯一性及在无挠结构附近的全局存在与光滑指数收敛。
    • 为G_2几何中的几何流提供稳定性理论和修正框架。
    💡 研究G_2几何中保持余结合子流形与瞬子的耦合流，证明修正系统的短时存在性与稳定性。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究紧G_2结构流形上在环境G_2结构演化时保持余结合子流形和G_2瞬子的耦合流。对余结合子流形，在环面上给出显式例子表明闭G_2结构的Laplacian流耦合平均曲率流即使任意接近平坦结构也可能无法保持余结合性。我们通过向环境方程添加沿子流形最小L^2范数椭圆修正的Lie导数来克服。证明从余结合初始数据出发修正耦合流的短时存在唯一性，无需刚性假设。还证明在环境上同调类固定时，对充分接近无挠余结合对的初始对，全局存在且光滑指数收敛。对瞬子，通过添加挠率项和沿指定余闭环境4-形式的Yang-Mills流的非局部椭圆修正提出类似修正耦合系统。证明在无环G_2瞬子附近联络的短时存在唯一性，并在无挠结构附近获得全局存在与光滑指数收敛。证明参数为零的修正余流在固定上同调类中无挠4-形式附近的全局存在与光滑指数收敛这一独立有趣结果。给出两个耦合系统的延拓准则并构造非零修正的紧例子。最后对每个正余流参数展示具有增长线性模的平坦环面，说明c=0是有意义的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study coupled flows on compact manifolds with $G_2$ structures that preserve coassociative submanifolds and $G_2$ instantons as the ambient $G_2$ structure evolves. For coassociatives, we give an explicit example on a torus showing that the Laplacian flow of closed $G_2$ structures coupled to mean curvature flow can fail to preserve coassociativity, even arbitrarily close to a flat structure. We circumvent this by adding a Lie derivative to the ambient equation, using an elliptic correction of minimum $L^2$ norm along the submanifold. We prove short-time existence and uniqueness of the corrected coupled flow from coassociative initial data, without any rigidity assumption. We also prove global existence and smooth exponential convergence for coassociative initial pairs sufficiently close to a torsion-free coassociative pair, with the ambient cohomology class fixed. For instantons, we propose a similar corrected coupled system by adding a torsion term and a nonlocal elliptic correction to Yang-Mills flow along prescribed coclosed ambient $4$-forms. We prove short-time existence and uniqueness of the corrected coupled system for connections nearby an acyclic $G_2$ instanton. We obtain global existence and smooth exponential convergence for the coupled corrected connection flow and the modified coflow of coclosed $G_2$ structures with parameter zero, near a torsion-free structure with an acyclic instanton. We prove a new result on the global existence and smooth exponential convergence near a torsion-free $4$-form in a fixed cohomology class for the modified coflow with $c=0$ which is a result of independent interest. We also give continuation criteria for both the coupled systems and construct compact examples with nonzero correction. Finally, for every positive coflow parameter, we exhibit a flat torus with a growing linear mode which shows why $c=0$ is the meaningful one.

    Rigidity for the quadratic Riemann curvature functional in dimensions greater than four
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究维数大于四时二次黎曼曲率泛函临界度量的刚性。
    • 在非紧流形上使用紧支变分并分析标量曲率与Cotton张量条件。
    • 证明有限曲率能量临界度量平坦或标量曲率严格负，共圆或弱Einstein条件下平坦。
    • 构造五维非平坦例子说明假设本质性。
    💡 研究维数大于四时二次黎曼曲率泛函临界度量的刚性并构造五维非平坦例子。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究维数大于四的黎曼流形上二次黎曼曲率泛函临界度量的刚性性质。该泛函是黎曼曲率张量平方范数的积分。在非紧流形上，临界性理解为紧支变分意义下，不假设有限总曲率能量。证明每个具有有限曲率能量的完备非紧临界度量要么平坦，要么标量曲率处处严格负。进一步证明每个具有共圆标量曲率的完备临界度量平坦，共圆性指标量曲率的Hessian逐点与度量成比例。因此每个具有无散度Cotton张量的完备弱Einstein临界度量平坦。后两个平坦性结果既不要求非紧性也不要求有限曲率能量。最后构造五维完备非平坦临界度量具有消失Cotton张量，表明共圆性和弱Einstein假设在各自平坦性结果中本质。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We investigate rigidity properties of critical metrics for the quadratic Riemann curvature functional on Riemannian manifolds of dimension greater than four. This functional is the integral of the squared norm of the Riemann curvature tensor. On noncompact manifolds, criticality is understood with respect to compactly supported variations, without assuming finite total curvature energy. We prove that every complete noncompact critical metric with finite curvature energy is either flat or has strictly negative scalar curvature everywhere. We further show that every complete critical metric with concircular scalar curvature is flat. Here concircularity means that the Hessian of the scalar curvature is pointwise proportional to the metric. Consequently, every complete weakly Einstein critical metric with divergence-free Cotton tensor is flat. The latter two flatness results require neither noncompactness nor finite curvature energy. Finally, we construct a complete nonflat critical metric in dimension five with vanishing Cotton tensor, showing that the concircularity and weakly Einstein assumptions are essential in the respective flatness results.

    • 研究8维2步幂零实李代数上复结构的存在性问题。

    • 证明40个类允许复结构，3个类存在障碍，并构造了所有偶维≥8的反例。
    • 结果推进了幂零李代数复结构的存在性理论，给出了首个高维反例。
    💡 确定8维2步幂零实李代数中哪些允许复结构，并构造高维反例。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文确定8维2步幂零实李代数中哪些允许（可积）复结构。我们在43个同构类中的40个上构造显式例子，并证明剩余3个类存在复结构障碍。这些障碍与定义李代数结构的铅笔中秩2元素集合有关。我们还证明对任意偶维李代数h，直和g=R^2⊕h（以及R^2由h的某些更一般扩张）允许复结构当且仅当h允许复结构。特别地，这构造了每个偶维2n≥8中不允许复结构的2步幂零李代数的首批例子。最后证明每个中心为2维的偶维2步幂零李代数均允许复结构。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper we determine which $8$-dimensional $2$-step nilpotent real Lie algebras admit (integrable) complex structures. We construct explicit examples on $40$ out of the $43$ isomorphism classes, and show that there are obstructions to the existence of complex structures on the remaining three isomorphism classes. These obstructions are related to the set of elements of rank $2$ in the pencil defining the Lie algebra structure. We also show that for any even-dimensional Lie algebra $\mathfrak{h}$, the direct sum $\mathfrak{g}:=\mathbb{R}^2\oplus \mathfrak{h}$ (and also some other, more general, extensions of $\mathbb{R}^2$ by $\mathfrak{h}$) admits a complex structure if and only if $\mathfrak{h}$ admits a complex structure. In particular, this allows us to construct the first examples of $2$-step nilpotent Lie algebras in every even dimension $2n\geq8$ which do not admit complex structures. Finally, we show that every even-dimensional $2$-step nilpotent Lie algebra with $2$-dimensional center admits a complex structure.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们提出一个基于轨迹的数学表述，用于模拟异质景观中野火锋面的传播，并应用于巴西潘塔纳尔。该表述由方向依赖的传播律和移动边界上的Finsler正交性组成，产生外向火锋轨迹的非线性局部系统。我们在演化边界的每一点提供严格凸的平移椭圆表示，保证所选外向速度的存在唯一性。依赖空间和小时风强迫的有效传播系数允许周界不规则演化，而不强加固定的全局锋面几何。计算轨迹经地理参考并与VIIRS活跃火观测进行各向同性空间配准。ERA5风和ERA5-Land植被数据用于描述环境强迫和景观异质性。2020年9月3-5日探索性实验的观测引导空间比较的全运行Dice系数分别为0.578、0.571和0.467。对空间传播系数、风速大小和速率律指数进行单次敏感性扰动，量化了环境强迫和参数变化对计算火势推进空间范围的主导影响。这些观察模式也指导了校准，在缺乏卫星采集精确时间戳的情况下，结果显示了事件内空间一致性而非独立预测验证。该框架为后续不确定性量化、多事件独立验证和模拟火蔓延的预期分区评估提供了数学严格的基础。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We present a mathematical formulation based on trajectories for modeling the propagation of wildfire fronts in heterogeneous landscapes and apply it to the Brazilian Pantanal. The formulation is made up of a direction-dependent propagation law and Finsler orthogonality along a moving boundary. This results in a nonlinear local system for outward fire front trajectories. We provide a strictly convex translated ellipse representation at each point of the evolving boundary which guaranties uniqueness and existence of the chosen outward velocity. The effective propagation coefficients that are dependent on space and the hourly wind forcing permit the perimeter to evolve irregularly without imposing a fixed global front geometry. Computed trajectories were georeferenced and isotropically spatially registered with VIIRS active-fire observations. ERA5 wind and ERA5-Land vegetation data were used to describe the environmental forcing and landscape heterogeneity. The full-run Dice coefficients for the observation-guided spatial comparisons for the exploratory experiments for September 3-5, 2020 are 0.578, 0.571, and 0.467 respectively. One-at-a-time sensitivity perturbations of the spatial propagation coefficient, wind speed magnitude and rate-law exponents quantify the governing influence of the environmental forcing and parameter variations on the spatial extent of the calculated fire advance. These observed patterns also guided calibration and, in the absence of exact timestamps of satellite acquisition, the results show within-event spatial agreement rather than independent predictive validation. The framework provides a mathematically rigorous basis for subsequent uncertainty quantification, independent validation over multiple events and prospective sector-wise assessments of simulated fire spread.

    对于R^n中给定凸体K和δ>0，我们引入一类新体，称为引力照明体I^δ(K)。我们展示其一些性质并探讨其与浮动体、照明体和Ulam浮动体的关系。意外现象发生，引力照明体表现出与浮动体、照明体和Ulam浮动体非常不同的行为。经典体都是凸的且包含于K或包含K，而新体不再如此。它们不必是凸的，但是星形的，且I^δ(K)可能与K及其补集均有非空交集。此外，包含行为可能随维数变化。最令人惊讶的是体积导数的行为：lim_{δ→0} |vol_n(K)-vol_n(I^δ(K))|/δ^{2/(n+1)}。在所有维数中，经典体的相应体积导数得到K的等仿射不变仿射表面积。对于引力照明体，除3维外所有维数也是如此。在3维中，该极限对所有凸体等于0。若用δ而非δ^{1/2}归一化，则在3维得到非零导数。非零极限是涉及凸体的仿射平均曲率和Pick不变量的新仿射不变量。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For a given convex body $K$ in $\mathbb R ^n$ and $\delta>0$, we introduce a new class of bodies, the {\em gravitational illumination bodies} $I^\delta(K)$. We show some of their properties and explore their relation to floating bodies, illumination bodies and Ulam floating bodies. Unexpected phenomena happen and the gravitational illumination bodies exhibit very different behavior than the floating bodies, illumination bodies and Ulam floating bodies. While the classical bodies are all convex and either contained in $K$ or containing $K$, this is no longer the case for the new bodies. They need not be convex, but they are star shaped and it may happen that $I^\delta(K)$ has non-empty intersection with both, $K$ and its complement. Moreover, the containment behavior may change with the dimension. Most surprising is the behavior of a volume derivative, $$ \lim_{\delta \to 0} \frac{ |\vol_n(K)-\vol_n(I^\delta(K)) |}{\delta^\frac{2}{n+1}}. $$ In all dimensions the corresponding volume derivative for the classical bodies results in the equi-affine invariant affine surface area of the body $K$. In the case of the gravitational illumination body, this is also the case in all dimensions, except in dimension $3$. In dimension $3$ the limit is equal to $0$ for all convex bodies. A non-zero derivative is obtained in dimension $3$ if one normalizes by $\delta$ instead of $\delta^\frac{1}{2}$. The non-zero limit is a new affine invariant involving the {\em affine mean curvature } and the {\em Pick invariant} of the convex body.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24618

    1997年，Catanese和第一作者证明，对每个m≥2，存在一个光滑闭4m维流形同时允许λ>0和λ<0的爱因斯坦度量；因此爱因斯坦常数λ的符号并不总是由宿主紧流形的微分拓扑决定。该结果后来被Răsdeaconu和Șuvaina（2009）改进，他们证明在每个4m维（m≥2）中实际上至少有C(m+3,3)个拓扑不同的此类例子。在本注记中，我们进一步改进这些先前结果，证明在每个4m维（m≥2）中实际上出现无穷多个拓扑不同的此类现象例子。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In 1997, Catanese and the first author showed that, for every $m\geq 2$, there exists a smooth closed $4m$-manifold that admits both a $\lambda > 0$ Einstein metric and a $\lambda < 0$ Einstein metric; thus, the sign of the Einstein constant $\lambda$ is not always determined by the differential topology of the host compact manifold. This result was later improved by R\u{a}sdeaconu and \c{S}uvaina (2009), who showed that there are in fact at least $\binom{m + 3}{3}$ topologically distinct such examples in each dimension $4m$, $m\geq 2$. In this note, we further improve these earlier results by showing that infinitely many topologically distinct examples of this phenomenon actually occur in dimension $4m$, for each $m\geq 2$.

    我们研究球面S^n和双曲空间H^n（n≥3）中在单参数等距群下按逆平均曲率流运动超曲面。没有闭超曲面具有此性质，常平均曲率孤子是 horosphere 的开子集。对于do Carmo和Dajczer的球型、双曲型和抛物型旋转超曲面，孤子方程化为单个常微分方程，我们在每个维数进行分析。在H^n中，horosphere出现在临界速度λ=1处，将整个碗与终止于奇异(n-2)-球的孤子分开；每类恰有一个horospherical端，它是渐近于全测地超曲面的端与终止于奇异轨道的端之间的阈值。在余维2的轴处得到完整三分法，在速度λ=2(n-1)处从对数螺线变为尖点边。在S^n中，通过轴的旋转孤子均不完备，其奇异球半径满足π/2-r_+=K_n/λ(1-2(n-1)^2λ^{-2}logλ)+O(λ^{-3})，其中K_n=√π Γ((n+1)/2)/Γ(n/2)。对于n=3，交换Killing场对给出另外两类：螺旋面和Hopf不变曲面；后者是圆S^2上逆曲线缩短流的旋转子。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study hypersurfaces of the sphere $\mathbb{S}^n$ and of hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^n$, $n\ge 3$, that move under the inverse mean curvature flow by a one-parameter group of isometries. No closed hypersurface has this property, and a soliton with constant mean curvature is an open subset of a horosphere. For the rotation hypersurfaces of do Carmo and Dajczer, of spherical, hyperbolic and parabolic type, the soliton equation reduces to a single ordinary differential equation, which we analyse in every dimension. In $\mathbb{H}^n$ the horosphere appears at the critical speed $\lambda=1$ and separates entire bowls from solitons that end at a singular $(n-2)$-sphere; each class has exactly one horospherical end, and it is the threshold between ends asymptotic to totally geodesic hypersurfaces and ends at a singular orbit. In $\mathbb{S}^n$ no rotational soliton through the axis is complete, and the radius of its singular sphere satisfies $\frac{\pi}{2}-r_+=\frac{K_n}{\lambda}\left(1-2(n-1)^2\lambda^{-2}\log\lambda\right)+O(\lambda^{-3})$, with $K_n=\sqrt{\pi}\,\Gamma(\frac{n+1}{2})/\Gamma(\frac{n}{2})$. For $n=3$, the commuting pairs of Killing fields give two further classes, helicoidal and Hopf-invariant surfaces; the latter are the rotators of the inverse curve shortening flow on the round two-sphere.

    Geometric convolutions on Riemannian manifolds depend on the geometry of the underlying domain, making stability under metric perturbations a fundamental question in geometric deep learning. This paper studies this question for parallel transport convolution through methods from differential geometry and analysis. The analysis develops perturbation estimates for geodesics, parallel transport, Jacobi fields, and exponential maps, and uses these estimates to compare the transported kernels. A central difficulty is the cut locus, which obstructs uniform comparison of the transport construction over the entire manifold. We address this difficulty by combining uniform estimates away from the cut locus with integral bounds on neighborhoods of arbitrarily small volume, while also controlling the change in the Riemannian volume measure.   30. [2611.03124] m-正形式和广义m-正形式的Hodge-Riemann关系

    💡 研究广义m-正形式的强正性与Hodge-Riemann关系，证明好前缀满足该关系。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究满足广义m-正性条件的实(1,1)-形式的强正性和Hodge-Riemann关系。我们证明由行列式逐次方向导数的双曲锥定义的每个好前缀具有严格强正乘积，并在每个双次数满足Hodge-Riemann关系。允许这些锥边界上的因子，我们证明每个非零前缀乘积的双曲锥等于其自然外代数商的多极化分量。这给出了普通m-正形式的完整混合Hodge-Riemann关系，无需半正性假设。我们还证明弱(m,l)-正形式的这些关系，当p+q≤m且max{p,q}≥l时。对于l=1和m≥2，关联的(m-1)次幂映射是从弱(m,1)-正锥到正(d-1,d-1)-锥的微分同胚。对于逐点满足普通、弱(m,l)-正或好前缀假设的光滑闭形式，Dinh-Nguyễn方法给出紧Kähler流形上相应的硬Lefschetz定理和Hodge-Riemann关系。我们还讨论数值准则、广义Hessian方程和广义m-正形式的相交不等式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study strong positivity and Hodge--Riemann relations for real $(1,1)$-forms satisfying generalized $m$-positivity conditions. We prove that every good prefix, defined by the hyperbolicity cones of successive directional derivatives of the determinant, has a strictly strongly positive product and satisfies the Hodge--Riemann relations in every bidegree. Allowing factors on the boundaries of these cones, we show that the hyperbolicity cone of every nonzero prefix product equals the polarizing component of its natural exterior-algebra quotient. This gives the full mixed Hodge--Riemann relations for ordinary $m$-positive forms without a semipositivity assumption. We also prove the relations for weakly $(m,l)$-positive forms, defined by $(m-l)$-positivity on every codimension-$l$ subspace, when $p+q\le m$ and $\max\{p,q\}\ge l$. For $l=1$ and $m\ge2$, the associated $(m-1)$-th power map is a diffeomorphism from the weak $(m,1)$-positive cone onto the positive $(d-1,d-1)$-cone. For smooth closed forms satisfying the ordinary, weak $(m,l)$-positive, or good-prefix hypotheses pointwise, Dinh--Nguy\^en's method gives the corresponding hard Lefschetz theorems and Hodge--Riemann relations on the cohomology of compact K\"ahler manifolds. We also discuss numerical criteria, generalized Hessian equations, and intersection inequalities for generalized $m$-positive forms.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03124

    We introduce the concept of a Fock bundle, a smooth principal bundle over a surface equipped with a special kind of adjoint-valued 1-form, as a new tool for studying character varieties of surface groups. Although similar to Higgs bundles, the crucial difference is that no complex structure is fixed on the underlying surface. Fock bundles are the gauge-theoretic realization of higher complex structures. We construct a canonical connection to a Fock bundle equipped with compatible symmetric pairing and hermitian structure. The space of flat Fock bundles maps to the character variety of the split real form. Determining the hermitian structure such that this connection is flat gives a non-linear PDE similar to Hitchin's equation. We explicitly construct solutions for Fock bundles in the Fuchsian locus. Ellipticity of the relevant linear operator provides a map from a neighborhood of the Fuchsian locus in the space of higher complex structures modulo higher diffeomorphisms to a neighborhood of the Fuchsian locus in the Hitchin component.

We also obtain two results for a real Banach space \(X\). \begin{list}{}{\setlength{\leftmargin}{\dimexpr24pt+1.8em\relax}\setlength{\rightmargin}{24pt}\setlength{\labelwidth}{1.4em}\setlength{\labelsep}{0.4em}\setlength{\topsep}{2pt}\setlength{\itemsep}{0pt}} \item[(a)] For \(1<p<2\), \(X\) has Enflo type \(p\) if and only if the Rademacher type \(p\) inequality holds uniformly over ordered families \((v_i)\) satisfying \[ v_i\perp_B v_j \qquad \text{for all } i<j, \] where \(\perp_B\) denotes Birkhoff--James orthogonality (that is, \(\|v_i+t v_j\|\ge\|v_i\|\) for every \(t\in\mathbb R\)). \item[(b)] For \(1<p\le2\), uniform validity of the Rademacher type \(p\) inequality on pairwise isosceles orthogonal families implies that \(X\) has Enflo type \(2p/(p+1)\). \end{list} These results show how Birkhoff--James and isosceles orthogonality reduce the linear estimates needed to establish a metric type property of the whole space.

We give sufficient conditions for an operator to be hereditarily universal and provide some examples. We also study $n$-tuples of operators by generalizing Vladimir M\"uller's results to the Banach setting. Finally, we obtain a result on the essential spectrum of a universal $n$-tuple of operators on $\ell_p$.

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study free products of vector lattices and their relation to free products of Banach lattices. We characterize the Archimedean vector lattices whose vector lattice free product with every Archimedean vector lattice is again Archimedean: these are precisely those whose finitely generated sublattices are projective. For finitely generated lattices, this connects the free-product property with polyhedral geometry through Baker--Beynon duality. We also show that the Banach lattice free product is the completion of the Archimedean vector lattice free product under the largest lattice norm making the canonical maps contractive. For finitely many factors, the Archimedean vector lattice free product is order dense in the Banach lattice free product. Moreover, it is shown that bounded families of injective lattice homomorphisms also induce injective homomorphisms between Banach lattice free products. Finally, in analogy with the theory of separably injective Banach lattices, we study relative injectivity and universal disposition for the class of vector lattices with countably many proper prime ideals. Every vector lattice embeds into one of universal disposition for this class, while ordered ultrapowers of the real line provide relatively injective examples of cardinality continuum.

    • 但特征空间包含连续多个不交开集，故非可分。

    💡 构造逆闭渐近球代数，其谱由可数求值特征生成但特征空间非可分，否定Ohrysko等问题。
    📖 中文摘要

    设X=∂B_2⊂C^2，R为球代数A(闭B_2)中函数在X上的限制。证明单位一致Banach代数A={(f_n)∈ℓ^∞(N,C(X)): dist_{C(X)}(f_n,R)→0}在ℓ^∞(N,C(X))中逆闭。因此一可数族求值特征生成A中每个元素的谱。然而Δ(A)包含连续多个两两不交非空开子集，故非可分。这否定Ohrysko、Wojciechowski和Graham提出的问题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $X=\partial\mathbb B_2\subset\mathbb C^2$ and let $\mathcal R$ be the restrictions to $X$ of functions in the ball algebra $A(\overline{\mathbb B_2})$. The unital uniform Banach algebra \[ \mathcal A=\{(f_n)\in\ell^\infty(\mathbb N,C(X)):\mathrm{dist}_{C(X)}(f_n,\mathcal R)\longrightarrow0\} \] is inverse-closed in $\ell^\infty(\mathbb N,C(X))$. Consequently one countable family of evaluation characters generates the spectrum of every element of $\mathcal A$. Nevertheless $\Delta(\mathcal A)$ contains continuum many pairwise disjoint nonempty open subsets and is therefore nonseparable. This gives a negative answer to a problem posed by Ohrysko, Wojciechowski and Graham.

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study endpoint estimates of Riesz transforms on a homogeneous tree equipped with its canonical flow measure $\mu$. We prove that the known boundedness from $H^1_{\mathrm{at}}(\mu)$ to $L^1(\mu)$ of the vertical Riesz transform can not be strengthened to boundedness on $H^1_{\mathrm{at}}(\mu)$ itself. We also prove that the adjoint vertical Riesz transform is not of weak type $(1,1)$. For the scalar horizontal transforms defined by nonzero bounded coefficients with zero sum on each child set, we establish $H^1_{\mathrm{at}}(\mu)\to L^1(\mu)$ boundedness and show that failure to preserve cancellation on atoms prevents boundedness on $H^1_{\mathrm{at}}(\mu)$. We then introduce a horizontal derivative based on deviations from sibling averages. Its associated Riesz transform retains the $L^p(\mu)$ bounds for $1<p<\infty$ and the weak type $(1,1)$ estimate, and is bounded on $H^1_{\mathrm{at}}(\mu)$ itself. These mapping properties parallel those of horizontal Riesz transforms in the continuous setting, supporting our construction as a natural discrete analogue. These results distinguish vertical and horizontal behaviour at the endpoint and identify cancellation on sibling sets as a mechanism for Hardy-space boundedness.

    📖 中文摘要

    地标扩散（Roseland）从度归一化数据到地标核矩阵的奇异值分解计算扩散映射型谱嵌入，成本远低于完全扩散映射，但假设所有数据一次可用。我们引入增量Roseland（iRoseland），将地标亲和矩阵视为行增长矩阵，用核化序列Karhunen-Loève（SKL）方案更新其SVD。度校正通过关于参考均值核行的一阶展开处理，使地标侧特征基可由每步单次地标对地标特征分解恢复。方法仅需单遍数据，有界内存运行且状态与处理点数无关。遗忘因子可跟踪漂移分布，样本量不变嵌入保持输出跨时间可比。实验表明在平稳数据上与Roseland一致到舍入误差，在长流上精度超过等内存Roseland一个数量级。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Landmark diffusion (Roseland) computes a diffusion-map-style spectral embedding from the singular value decomposition of a degree-normalized data-to-landmark kernel matrix, at a cost far below that of full diffusion maps. It assumes, however, that all data are available at once. We introduce incremental Roseland (iRoseland), which casts the landmark affinity as a row-growing matrix whose SVD can be updated with a kernelized Sequential Karhunen--Lo\`eve (SKL) scheme. Degre correction is handled by a first order expansion about a reference mean kernel row and so that the landmark-side eigenbasis may be recovered from a single landmark-by-landmark eigendecomposition at each step. The presented method requires only a single pass over the data and runs in bounded-memory with state independent of the number of points processed. A forgetting factor lets the method track drifting distributions, and a sample-size-invariant embedding keeps outputs comparable across time. Experiments show agreement with Roseland to round-off on stationary data, and accuracy that exceeds equal-memory Roseland by an order of magnitude on long streams. On drifting data, we observe large gains from forgetting with a window matched to the drift.

    📖 中文摘要

    Monge-Kantorovich最优传输是确定将一个概率分布变换为另一个同时最小化成本函数的最有效方式的数学框架。其核心构造包括Wasserstein距离和对偶表述。标准量子推广用密度算子替换概率分布、用半正定成本算子替换成本函数，应视为空间量子最优传输。本文通过时空相空间Kirkwood-Dirac准概率分布和时空态提出时空量子最优传输问题。证明两种表述等价并通过时空算子框架相联系。在Kirkwood-Dirac表述中，时空分布和成本函数为复值，耦合为给出正确边缘的准概率分布；在时空态表述中，成本算子一般非Hermite，耦合一般非Hermite并给出正确约化局部态。Margenau-Hill分布给出实值时空相空间最优传输。还引入满足度量公理的时空量子Wasserstein度量。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Monge-Kantorovich optimal transport is a mathematical framework that determines the most efficient way to transform one probability distribution into another while minimizing a given cost function. Its central constructions include the Wasserstein distances, which quantify the space of probability distributions, and dual formulations that link to convex analysis. Its standard quantum generalization replaces probability distributions with density operators and cost function with positive semidefinite cost operator, which should be regarded as spatial quantum optimal transport. In this work, we propose the quantum optimal transport problem in spacetime through the spatiotemporal phase-space Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability distribution and spatiotemporal states and through spatiotemporal state. We show that the two formalisms are equivalent and related via spatiotemporal operator frames. In the Kirkwood-Dirac phase-space formalism, the spatiotemporal distribution and the cost function are complex-valued, and the spatiotemporal couplings are Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability distributions that yield the correct marginals for the local states. In the spatiotemporal state formalism, the cost operators are generally non-Hermitian, and the spatiotemporal couplings are generally non-Hermitian and yield the correct reduced local states. The Margenau-Hill phase-space distribution gives rise to a real-valued optimal transport in spatiotemporal phase space; in the spatiotemporal state formalism, this corresponds to a Hermitian spatiotemporal coupling state. We give a rigorous definitions and a careful discussion of its duality. We also introduce a spatiotemporal quantum Wasserstein metric based on a metric in spatiotemporal phase spaces and show that it satisfies the metric axioms.

    💡 证明球面和欧氏空间上旋转不变有限可加概率测度的唯一性，解决Borel Ruziewicz问题。
    📖 中文摘要

    证明对n≥2，归一化曲面测度是S^n的Borel子集上唯一的旋转不变有限可加概率测度；对d≥3，Lebesgue测度是R^d有界Borel子集上在保向等距下不变且单位立方体上归一化的唯一有限可加测度。这回答了Ruziewicz问题的Borel版本。在S^n上只需假设在一组具有谱间隙的有限旋转下不变，在R^d上只需关于两个不同中心的两组此类旋转不变。主要步骤是给出不变有限可加概率测度在零集上消失的准则。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that for $n\ge2$ the normalized surface measure is the only rotation-invariant finitely additive probability measure on the Borel subsets of $\mathbb S^n$, and that for $d\ge3$ Lebesgue measure is the only finitely additive measure on the bounded Borel subsets of $\mathbb{R}^d$ which is invariant under orientation-preserving isometries and normalized on the unit cube. This answers the Borel version of the Ruziewicz problem. On $\mathbb S^n$ it suffices to assume invariance under a finite set of rotations with a spectral gap, and on $\mathbb{R}^d$ under two such sets of rotations about two distinct centres. The main step is a criterion for invariant finitely additive probability measures to vanish on null sets. For a finite nonempty symmetric set of homeomorphisms of a $\sigma$-compact metrizable space with a quasi-invariant Borel measure $m$ which is finite on compact sets, such as Lebesgue measure on $\mathbb{R}^d$, every invariant finitely additive probability measure on Borel sets vanishes on $m$-null sets if and only if there is $\delta>0$ such that every countably additive Borel probability measure supported on an $m$-null Borel set is moved by some generator by at least $\delta$ in total variation. Sufficiency is proved by constructing, for every invariant null Borel set, a bounded Borel field on the orbit graph, supported on the set and with divergence at least one there.

For moment functions localized at scale $r_n$, we prove an estimate controlling $W_\infty$ by $W_p$ up to an additive error of order $r_n$, which also applies to discrete measures. For repulsive costs, we show that minimizers remain separated from the diagonal, uniformly in the number of moments, and we derive consequences for dual potentials. Finally, under a moment-correction assumption, we prove that the fermionic minimum energies converge in the semiclassical limit to the moment-constrained Coulomb transport value for a fixed family of moment functions.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24688

    Whitehead doubles and the failure of the AJ conjecture
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究环面纽结的标记Whitehead双纽结的AJ猜想。
    • 证明在多种参数下AJ猜想失效。
    • 分析有色Jones序列的零化子与A-多项式差异。
    • 并利用卫星转移算子解释该差异。
    💡 证明互素参数的环面纽结的标记Whitehead双纽结在多种情形下AJ猜想失效，并分析其A-多项式差异。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于互素参数2≤r<s的环面纽结的标记Whitehead双纽结，AJ猜想在两种扣环符号、两种伴随手性和每个整数扭转下均失效。零标架带符号未约化有色Jones序列具有非零有理饱和零化子。每个非零本原代表在t=-1处非平凡特殊化，且经线变量M独立。以L为经线变量，特殊化包含负扣环的不可约二次式E=L^2-(M^4-4M^2+1)L+M^4和正扣环的M^4E(M^{-1},L)。相应的二次式与完整经典A-多项式（包括L-1）在Q(M)[L^{±1}]上互素，因此即使每个不可约因子只计一次，差异仍存在。曲线E=0来自标记Whitehead链环外部及其轴尖点完备化。对于所示首一多项式，πm(E)是该双尖点外部的无向体积。我们使用实际Jones序列选择卫星转移算子的右因子。有理因子检验表明二次式在该因子中存活，而两个标记伴随填充将其排除为卫星边界曲线的分量。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For marked Whitehead doubles of torus knots with coprime parameters $2\le r<s$, the AJ conjecture fails for both clasp signs, both companion chiralities, and every integer twist. The zero-framed signed unreduced colored Jones sequence has a nonzero rationally saturated annihilator. Every nonzero primitive representative specializes nontrivially at $t=-1$ with the meridian variable $M$ independent. With $L$ the longitude variable, the specialization contains the irreducible quadratic $E=L^2-(M^4-4M^2+1)L+M^4$ for the negative clasp and $M^4E(M^{-1},L)$ for the positive clasp. The corresponding quadratic is coprime to the full classical $A$-polynomial, including $L-1$, over $\mathbb{Q}(M)[L^{\pm1}]$, so the discrepancy persists when each irreducible factor is counted once. The curve $E=0$ comes from the marked Whitehead link exterior with its axis cusp complete. For the displayed monic polynomial, $\pi m(E)$ is the unsigned volume of that two-cusped exterior. We use the actual Jones sequence to select a right divisor of the satellite transfer operator. Rational factor tests show that the quadratic survives in this divisor, and the two marked companion fillings exclude it as a component of the satellite boundary curve.

We also prove the existence of a localization spectral sequence relating the symplectic Khovanov--Rozansky homology of a two-periodic link to the symplectic Khovanov-Rozansky homology of the quotient link, in analogy to a theorem of Seidel and Smith for symplectic Khovanov homology. The proofs rely on a generalization of the localization theorem of Seidel-Smith and Large for equivariant Lagrangian Floer cohomology with respect to order two actions; in particular, we extend the theory to the case that the fixed set of the action carries a stable normal real polarization.

    💡 研究双曲群视觉边界上的对数重规范，证明其与Floyd边界度量双Lipschitz等价并分析维数性质。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究视觉边界的一种对数重规范，它源于Garg-Jana-Qing在次线性Morse边界上引入的对数度量。对每个真测地双曲空间X，我们证明d_log(ξ,η) ≍ (1+(ξ|η)_o)^{-θ}。作为推论，在边界的自然等同下，d_log与径向密度g_θ(t)=(1+t)^{-1-θ}对应的多项式Floyd边界度量双Lipschitz等价。真测地双曲空间间的每个拟等距诱导其对数边界间具有固定容许指数的双Lipschitz映射。对每个非初等双曲群，对数边界是非倍分的，且Hausdorff和Assouad维数无限。然而，对数重规范保持Nagata（容量）维数，并继续恢复群的渐近维数。它还通过线性连通性（有界转折）检测单端性，并保持一致完全性。因此，对数度量与视觉度量不是拟对称等价的，尽管若干粗几何特征在对数边界中仍可见。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study a logarithmic re-gauging of the visual boundary, arising from the logarithmic metric on the sublinear Morse boundary introduced by Garg-Jana-Qing. For every proper geodesic hyperbolic space $X$, we prove that $d_{\mathrm{log}}(\xi,\eta)\asymp (1+(\xi\mid\eta)_o)^{-\theta}$. As a consequence, under the natural identification of boundaries, $d_{\mathrm{log}}$ is bi-Lipschitz equivalent to the polynomial Floyd boundary metric associated with the radial density $g_\theta(t)=(1+t)^{-1-\theta}$. Every quasi-isometry between proper geodesic hyperbolic spaces induces a bi-Lipschitz map between their logarithmic boundaries with a fixed admissible exponent. For every non-elementary hyperbolic group, the logarithmic boundary is nondoubling and has infinite Hausdorff and Assouad dimensions. Nevertheless, the logarithmic re-gauging preserves Nagata (capacity) dimension and continues to recover the asymptotic dimension of the group. It also detects one-endedness through linear connectedness (bounded turning) and preserves uniform perfectness. Thus the logarithmic and visual metrics are not quasisymmetrically equivalent, although several coarse-geometric features remain visible in the logarithmic boundary.

    我们通过考虑从圆到穿孔复平面、卷绕数为+1的实解析映射来研究圆微分同胚群的复化问题。这些复形变构成无限维流形，具有部分定义的求逆和复合运算，在Frélicher结构意义下光滑，且在恒等处的Lie代数关系由Witt代数给出。我们计算其实系数第二群上同调。它由两个上循环生成：一个扩展与Virasoro代数的Gelf'and-Fuks上循环相关的Bott-Thurston上循环，另一个是结合平均旋转和共形半径的Euler类上循环。复形变自然作用在具有枚举和解析参数化边界分支的Riemann曲面的（无限维）Segal模空间上，赋予其光滑Frélicher结构。我们证明Virasoro均匀化定理：Segal模空间的切空间由Witt代数诱导的向量场张成。最后，我们将复形变的作用与Fenchel-Nielsen坐标和有限维带边双曲曲面模空间上的Schiffer变分联系起来。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We approach the question of complexification of the diffeomorphism group of the circle by considering real-analytic maps from the circle into the punctured complex plane with winding number +1. Such complex deformations form an infinite-dimensional manifold with partially defined inversion and composition operations, smooth in the sense of Fr\"olicher structures, and with Lie algebra relations at the identity given by the Witt algebra. We compute their second group cohomology with real coefficients. It is generated by two cocycles: One cocycle extending the Bott-Thurston cocycle related to the Gelf'and-Fuks cocycle of the Virasoro algebra, and another Euler class cocycle combining mean rotation and conformal radius. Complex deformations act naturally on the (infinite-dimensional) Segal moduli spaces of Riemann surfaces with enumerated and analytically parametrized boundary components, equipping them with smooth Fr\"olicher structures. We prove a Virasoro uniformization theorem: The tangent spaces of the Segal moduli spaces are spanned by vector fields induced by the Witt algebra. Finally, we relate the actions of complex deformations to Fenchel-Nielsen coordinates and Schiffer variation on finite-dimensional moduli spaces of bordered hyperbolic surfaces with one marked point on each boundary component.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20175

We also prove that if $A$ has finite order and the exponent of convergence of its zeros, counted with multiplicity, is smaller than its order, then the zeros of every non-trivial solution have infinite exponent of convergence.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们分类任意真平面区域间的满拟双曲等距。除欧氏相似外，保向等距是扇形或穿孔平面上的倒数映射以及对数螺线扇形上的显式复幂映射。特别地，保向平面拟双曲等距不必是Möbius变换。作为推论，当任一区域具有有限内半径、对于除穿孔平面外的多连通区域、以及对于除半平面外的$C^1$区域时，我们得到相似刚性。$C^1$结论加强了Hästö关于平面$C^3$区域的相应结果。我们的平面分类与Richard在$n\geq3$维的分类一起，完全解决了$\mathbb R^n$（$n\geq2$）真子区域间的拟双曲等距问题。作为应用，我们将Herron、Richard和Snipes关于拟双曲区域点化Gromov-Hausdorff收敛的刻画推广到二维。

    🌐 English Abstract

The $C^1$ conclusion strengthens H\"ast\"o's corresponding result for planar $C^3$ domains. Our planar classification, together with Richard's classification in dimensions $n\geq3$, completely solves the quasihyperbolic isometry problem between proper subdomains of $\mathbb R^n$ in every dimension $n\geq2$. As an application, we extend to dimension two the characterization of pointed Gromov--Hausdorff convergence of quasihyperbolic domains obtained by Herron, Richard, and Snipes.

    • 研究SLE焊接测度在复合下的拟不变性。

    对$\kappa\in(0,4]$，我们证明SLE$_\kappa$环测度诱导的焊接律的Cameron-Martin定理。在左右同时复合$h\mapsto\varphi\circ h\circ\psi^{-1}$下，变换后的律与原律相互绝对连续当且仅当两个映射都属于Weil-Petersson群，否则相互奇异。因此Weil-Petersson群是焊接律在任一侧复合下的拟不变群。我们还证明它在保向圆周同胚群中等于自身的正规化子。对Weil-Petersson对，我们导出用SLE焊接良定义的相对作用表示的显式Radon-Nikodym导数，并证明它属于$L^{1+\varepsilon}$，其中$\varepsilon>0$依赖于该对。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For $\kappa\in(0,4]$, we prove a Cameron--Martin theorem for the welding law induced by SLE$_\kappa$ loop measure. Under simultaneous left and right composition $h\mapsto\varphi\circ h\circ\psi^{-1}$, the transformed law is mutually absolutely continuous with respect to the original law exactly when both maps belong to the Weil--Petersson group, and is mutually singular otherwise. Thus the Weil--Petersson group is the quasi-invariance group of the welding law under composition on either side. We also prove that it is its own normalizer in the group of orientation-preserving circle homeomorphisms. For Weil--Petersson pairs, we derive an explicit Radon--Nikodym derivative in terms of a relative action well defined for SLE weldings and prove that it belongs to $L^{1+\varepsilon}$ for some $\varepsilon>0$ depending on the pair.

    我们证明，对每个$n\ge9$和所有总权重为2的正有理权重，$\mathbb P^1$上$n$个有序点的GIT商不同构于算术复球商的Baily-Borel紧化。权重可位于稳定性墙上，格可有挠，商映射可沿除子分歧。不假设与给定周期映射相容。我们研究从拉回到完备曲线的切丛到负次数线丛的同态，并将所得限制与算术格中有限反射群不动球像的法丛比较。通过重合标记点描述这些同态并证明其核的消没。结合Chern类的整性和有限反射群的不变量理论，在不假设GIT商Fano的条件下得到不存在性定理。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that, for every $n\ge9$ and all positive rational weights smaller than one with total weight two, the GIT quotient of $n$ ordered points on $\mathbb P^1$ is not isomorphic to the Baily--Borel compactification of an arithmetic complex ball quotient. The weights may lie on stability walls, and the lattice may have torsion; the quotient map may be branched along divisors. No compatibility with a prescribed period map is assumed. We study homomorphisms from the tangent bundle pulled back to complete curves into line bundles of negative degree and compare the resulting constraints with normal bundles of the images of balls fixed by finite reflection groups in the arithmetic lattice. We describe these homomorphisms through coincident marked points and prove vanishing statements for their kernels. The comparison, together with integrality of Chern classes and the invariant theory of finite reflection groups, gives the nonexistence theorem without a Fano assumption on the GIT quotient.

    📖 中文摘要

    对任意维欧氏空间中的每个有限点集$X$，我们证明存在最大度为三且总长度至多$(757/500)\MST(X)=1.514\MST(X)$的生成树，其中$\MST(X)$表示无限制最小生成树长度。更强地，对任意根和任意有限其他点列表，存在从根出发的Hamilton路径，其长度至多是最小根到点距离和的1.514倍。证明遵循先前工作中的有根路径和可变块框架，并对路径的显式有理混合应用Young的加权距离不等式。八个有序半径区域覆盖归一化区域。其局部估计仅需29个顶点比较，全部由有理算术验证。覆盖使用三个阈值$1/4$、$3/8$和$1/2$；最大块有九个顶点。我们给出归一化转移、全局归纳和零或一个实际尾点的终端情形的完整证明。我们还给出固定维交叉多面体下界，以显式坐标呈现Zbarsky的正交单纯簇构造，并比较其有限维界。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For every finite point set $X$ in a Euclidean space of arbitrary dimension, we prove that there is a spanning tree of maximum degree three and total length at most $(757/500)\MST(X)=1.514\MST(X)$, where $\MST(X)$ denotes the unrestricted minimum spanning-tree length. More strongly, for any root and any finite list of other points, there is a Hamiltonian path starting at the root whose length is at most $1.514$ times the sum of the root-to-point distances. The proof follows the rooted-path and variable-block framework used in earlier work, and applies Young's weighted-distance inequality to explicit rational mixtures of paths. Eight ordered radius regions cover the normalized domain. Their local estimates require only 29 vertex comparisons, all certified by rational arithmetic. The cover uses the three thresholds $1/4$, $3/8$, and $1/2$; its largest block has nine vertices. We give complete proofs of the normalization transfer, the global induction, and the terminal cases with zero or one actual tail point. We also give a fixed-dimensional cross-polytope lower bound, present Zbarsky's orthogonal simplex-cluster construction in explicit coordinates, and compare their finite-dimension bounds. The base cases complete the upper-bound argument before the lower-bound constructions are presented.

    📖 中文摘要

    在先前工作（arXiv:2603.21158）中，我们为晶界处邻近重合点集引入了模糊旋转对称性。一个给定旋转必须在每个对容差$\varepsilon$下，通过将每个允许原子对的中点移动至多$\varepsilon/2$（最大对间距的一半）来实现。我们证明体心立方$[100]$扭转晶界平面模型的所提分类。在$0\leq\theta\leq45^\circ$上，最大旋转阶数在$30^\circ$为十二，在$45^\circ$为八，在其他取向差处为四。模糊$n$重对称迫使Fourier模$M$满足$\xi_n M=M$，从而在晶体坐标中$\mathbb{Z}[\xi_n]\subseteq M$。对每个保持$M$的有限阶旋转，我们确定对所有足够小$\varepsilon$的最小均匀位移，允许独立点运动和自由旋转中心。在此区域，$30^\circ$的十二重对称代价为$\varepsilon/(2\sqrt3)$，$45^\circ$的八重对称代价为零。在约$25.53^\circ$的扭转处，Fourier模也具有八重对称，但在平面中实现它代价为$\sqrt2\,\varepsilon$。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In earlier work (arXiv:2603.21158) we introduced vague rotational symmetry for proximal coincidence point sets at grain boundaries. A prescribed rotation must be realizable at every pair tolerance $\varepsilon$ by moving each midpoint of an admitted atomic pair by at most $\varepsilon/2$, half the maximum pair separation. We prove the proposed classification for the planar model of a body-centred cubic $[100]$ twist boundary. On $0\leq\theta\leq45^\circ$, the maximal rotational order is twelve at $30^\circ$, eight at $45^\circ$, and four at every other misorientation. Vague $n$-fold symmetry forces $\xi_n M=M$ for the Fourier module $M$, hence $\mathbb{Z}[\xi_n]\subseteq M$ in crystal coordinates. For every rotation of finite order preserving $M$, we determine the least uniform displacement for all sufficiently small $\varepsilon$, allowing independent point motions and a free rotation centre. In this regime twelvefold symmetry at $30^\circ$ costs $\varepsilon/(2\sqrt3)$, and eightfold symmetry at $45^\circ$ costs zero. At a twist of approximately $25.53^\circ$, the Fourier module also has eightfold symmetry, but realizing it in the plane costs $\sqrt2\,\varepsilon$.
