  5. [2610.40703] Hibi--Li (2016) 猜想被完全解决
    The Hibi--Li (2016) conjecture is fully resolved
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Hibi-Li关于序多面体与链多面体面数比较的猜想。
    • 通过分析含五元偏序集X的偏序集，利用Fourier的预印本结果给出面数差的显式下界。
    • 证明当P含X时链多面体面数严格更多，并构造达到所有界的例子。
    • 结合k=1的反例，完全解决Hibi-Li猜想。
    💡 完全解决Hibi-Li猜想，证明含五元偏序集X时链多面体面数严格更多且等号情形不成立。
    📖 中文摘要

    Hibi和Li猜想：有限偏序集P的序多面体O(P)在每一维的面数至多与其链多面体C(P)相同，且某维k（1≤k≤|P|-1）的等号迫使两多面体幺模等价。Fourier在2026年预印本中证明了不等式。设X为五元偏序集，其中一元素位于两个不可比元素之上、另两个不可比元素之下。利用Fourier预印本的两个结果，我们证明：若n元偏序集包含X为诱导子偏序集，则对每个2≤k≤n-1，其链多面体比序多面体至少多binom(n-3,k-2)个k维面；且对每个n≥5，某含X的n元偏序集同时达到所有这些界。由于Hibi和Li已证明O(P)与C(P)幺模等价当且仅当P不含X，这证明了这些k值下的等号陈述。Fourier预印本第二版给出该陈述的独立证明。对k=1，等号陈述不成立：O(X)与C(X)边数相同（Hibi, Li, Sahara和Shikama）但非幺模等价。结合Fourier的不等式，这些结果解决了该猜想。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Hibi and Li conjectured that the order polytope $\mathcal{O}(P)$ of a finite poset $P$ has at most as many faces of each dimension as its chain polytope $\mathcal{C}(P)$, and that equality in some dimension $k$ with $1\leq k\leq|P|-1$ forces the two polytopes to be unimodularly equivalent. Fourier proved the inequality in a 2026 preprint. Let $X$ be the five-element poset in which an element lies above two incomparable elements and below two other incomparable elements. Using two results from Fourier's preprint, we show that if an $n$-element poset contains $X$ as an induced subposet, then, for every $k$ with $2\leq k\leq n-1$, its chain polytope has at least $\binom{n-3}{k-2}$ more $k$-dimensional faces than its order polytope, and that for every $n\geq5$ some $n$-element poset containing $X$ attains all these bounds at once. Since Hibi and Li showed that $\mathcal{O}(P)$ and $\mathcal{C}(P)$ are unimodularly equivalent exactly when $P$ does not contain $X$, this proves the equality statement for these values of $k$. The second version of Fourier's preprint gives an independent proof of this statement. For $k=1$, the equality statement is false: the polytopes $\mathcal{O}(X)$ and $\mathcal{C}(X)$ have the same number of edges (Hibi, Li, Sahara and Shikama) but are not unimodularly equivalent. Together with Fourier's inequality, these results settle the conjecture.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40703

    🌐 English Abstract

Divided-power spikes are basis elements dual to polynomial monomials whose exponents have the form $2^j-1$ for nonnegative integers $j$. Their translates under ${\rm GL}_4(\mathbb F_2)$, the group of invertible $4\times4$ matrices over the field with two elements, generate submodules of the Steenrod kernel, the subspace annihilated by all positive Steenrod squares. Identifying the relation spaces in these presentations gives explicit equivariant comparison isomorphisms.     📖 中文摘要

    We prove an explicit stability line for the homology $H_i(\mathrm{Hur}_n^c,\mathbb{Q})$ of any Hurwitz space associated to a finite rack $c$ depending only on its homology up to some finite $n$. Thus, for any Hurwitz space, a finite computation will yield near-optimal bounds on secondary terms for the corresponding arithmetic statistics problem. Our theorem also applies more generally to higher order stability, Frobenius weights, and braided vector spaces. As a special case of our theorem, we can deduce stability lines depending only on when $H_0(\mathrm{Hur}^c_n,\mathbb{Q})$ stabilizes, which are sharp in some cases. We apply this to two families of Hurwitz spaces to obtain bounds on the secondary term for (1) the number of $S_d$-degree $d$ function fields with squarefree discriminant, and (2) Cohen-Lenstra moments.

  11. [2611.24613] 作为时间超图拓扑透镜的儿童画
    Dessins d'Enfants as a Topological Lens on Temporal Hypergraphs
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究时间超图的拓扑表示，考虑顶点和超边的循环序。
    • 利用儿童画理论和Walsh对应，将超图嵌入曲面并定义亏格。
    • 证明插入操作对应循环闭合事件，给出可解释的循环组织度量。
    • 应用于Stack Exchange社区，区分标准统计无法区分的社区。
    💡 利用Grothendieck儿童画理论为时间超图构造曲面，以顶点序和超边序编码循环结构。
    📖 中文摘要

    超图是群体交互的自然语言，拓扑数据分析提供了几种观察它们的方式：单纯复形和胞腔复形、持久同调以及层。所有这些都将超边视为无序集合。本文引入一种不同的、据我们所知是新的视角，来自Grothendieck的儿童画理论，与这些方法不同，它考虑顶点在每个超边中出现的顺序以及超边在每个顶点周围出现的顺序。儿童画是画在闭可定向曲面上的二部图。通过Walsh关于超映射与二部映射的经典对应，每个在每个顶点和每个超边内具有关联循环序的超图都是一个儿童画，因此确定一个带面和亏格的曲面。我们为时间超图发展这一想法，通过重放历史构建曲面：关联按发生顺序添加，每个新关联插入到迄今构建的循环序中，紧邻与其相关的较早关联。我们证明每次插入要么不产生循环地增长超图，要么通过分裂一个面局部闭合循环，要么通过添加一个环柄全局闭合循环；独立循环数等于局部加环柄事件数，亏格等于环柄事件数。这些计数给出可解释的、大小受控的度量，衡量时间超图的循环在增长时如何在曲面上组织。我们将方法应用于五个Stack Exchange社区，表明它能区分标准统计无法区分的社区，并量化真实交互顺序的重要性。我们进一步讨论曲面视角对现有高阶网络分析的补充，以及它如何开辟基于儿童画的超图学习路径。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Hypergraphs are the natural language for group interactions, and topological data analysis has given us several ways to look at them: simplicial and cell complexes, persistent homology, and sheaves. All of these treat a hyperedge as an unordered set. In this paper, we introduce a different and, to our knowledge, new viewpoint that comes from Grothendieck's theory of dessins d'enfants and, unlike these approaches, takes into account the order in which vertices appear in each hyperedge and hyperedges appear around each vertex. A dessin is a bipartite graph drawn on a closed orientable surface. Through Walsh's classical correspondence between hypermaps and bipartite maps, every hypergraph equipped with a cyclic order of incidences at each vertex and inside each hyperedge is a dessin, and therefore determines a surface with faces and a genus. We develop this idea for temporal hypergraphs and build the surface by replaying history: incidences are added in the order in which they occurred, and each new incidence is inserted into the cyclic orders built so far, next to the earlier incidence to which it is related. We prove that every insertion either grows the hypergraph without creating a cycle, closes a cycle locally by splitting a face, or closes it globally by adding a handle; the number of independent cycles equals the number of local plus handle events, and the genus equals the number of handle events. These counts give interpretable, size-controlled measures of how a temporal hypergraph's cycles organize on a surface as it grows. We apply our method to five Stack Exchange communities and show that it separates communities that standard statistics cannot tell apart and quantifies how much the real order of interactions matters. We further discuss what the surface viewpoint adds to existing higher-order network analysis and how it opens a path toward dessin-based learning on hypergraphs.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24613

    📖 中文摘要

    设f:(C^n,0)→(C,0)是原点具有孤立临界点的全纯芽，φ_f是Re f关于光滑黎曼度量的局部负梯度流。我们计算点化同伦Conley指标h(φ_f,{0})≃ΣF_f≃∨_{j=1}^{μ(f)}S^n，其中F_f是Milnor纤维，Σ表示约化悬垂。该指标与度量无关，其约化整同调在n次有秩μ(f)，其余为零。证明利用连续形变到全纯Morse化。Hermitian度量下虚部守恒允许目标旋转以排除连接轨道，从而同伦层面的和性质适用。我们还给出齐次和加权齐次函数的球面指标对，描述二次分裂，并通过McCord定理恢复Poincaré--Hopf指标。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $f:(\mathbb{C}^n,0)\to(\mathbb{C},0)$ be a holomorphic germ with an isolated critical point at the origin, and let $\varphi_f$ be the local negative gradient flow of $\operatorname{Re}f$ for a smooth Riemannian metric. We compute the pointed homotopy Conley index $h(\varphi_f,\{0\})\simeq\Sigma F_f\simeq\bigvee_{j=1}^{\mu(f)}S^n$, where $F_f$ is the Milnor fiber and $\Sigma$ denotes reduced suspension. The index is independent of the metric, and its reduced integral homology has rank $\mu(f)$ in degree $n$ and vanishes otherwise. The proof uses continuation to a holomorphic morsification. Conservation of the imaginary part for a Hermitian metric allows a target rotation to exclude connecting orbits, so the homotopy-level sum property applies. We also give spherical index pairs for homogeneous and weighted homogeneous functions, describe quadratic splitting, and recover the Poincar\'e--Hopf index through McCord's theorem.
